### sample VideoProcessor.cfg
# VideoProcessor release sample configuration
#
# This file is read at startup from the same directory as VideoProcessor.exe.
# Leave a setting commented out unless it is appropriate for your hardware.

[general]
# VP-0179 recovery policy (Config: Startup). Restart VP after changing this value.
# false: legacy invalid-state recovery, including frame-counter retention.
# true (default): bounded 1500 ms grace; only valid capture state cancels recovery.
bounded_invalid_capture_recovery: true

# The portable internal baseline starts visibly on the first Quad HDMI input
# with VP Renderer. Change either value in Config for different hardware.
capture_device: DeckLink Quad HDMI Recorder (1)
# Optional connection type for devices that expose more than one connection.
# A Quad HDMI channel normally supports only HDMI, so this may stay omitted.
#capture_input: HDMI
# VP Renderer uses the promoted master-based fork and is the default.
# VP Renderer (Legacy) keeps 7.360.1 and must be selected explicitly.
# Existing VP Renderer and VP Renderer (Beta) selections load VP Renderer.
renderer: VP Renderer
# Hide legacy DirectShow renderer registrations from the Windows system folders.
hide_legacy_renderers: true
fullscreen: false
windowed_fullscreen_mode: false
noui: false
startminimized: false
#fullscreen_monitor_name: <Windows monitor friendly name>
#fullscreen_monitor_session_mode: target-only
scene_detect: false
switch_refresh_rate: true
# VP Renderer only: submit on every display refresh, repeating the processed
# image as needed. Independent of smooth motion; disabled by default.
present_every_vsync: false
# Maximum pending output presentations (1..8); independent of capture Queue.
presentation_queue_depth: 2
# Total time for the profile-change overlay, including its fade. 0 disables it.
profile_change_display_seconds: 5
# VP Renderer DirectShow filter (local files in MPC-HC / MPC-BE) only:
# picture offset against the audio in ms, -2000 to 2000 (positive = later).
#file_av_offset_ms: 0
# Keep Dynamic Drive running this long between files so the projector does not flash
# to full light while the player rebuilds its graph; 0 to 30000 ms, 0 = off.
#file_led_holdover_ms: 3000
# Shared input-processing defaults. Leave a renderer-owned value omitted to
# inherit these values; set it under [directshow] or [vprenderer.input_processing] only when
# that backend needs a different ingress policy.
#video_conversion: V210_TO_P010
#container_colorspace: BT2020
hdr_colorspace: FOLLOW_INPUT
hdr_luminance: FOLLOW_INPUT
# Treat BT.2020 without an HDR flag as HDR (LLDV/HDR10): a BT.2020, 10/12-bit
# signal without an HDR InfoFrame is shown as PQ after 1.5 s, metadata from the
# [lldv] profile. The SDR veto (default on) returns real SDR to SDR.
#newlldv: true
#newlldv_sdr_veto: true
# TV-led Dolby Vision RGB tunnel from Vertex2/DeckLink r210 to VP Renderer.
# Requires a real Dolby Vision EDID and no Vertex2 scaling.
#dolby_vision_tunnel: true

# The first profile in each group is the default. Alternate profiles are
# chosen in Config or by when: rules; the shortcut: keys shipped below are only
# a backup for A/B comparison. A key's choice lasts until restart or a profile
# change ([general] persist_profile_selection: true keeps it across restarts).

# Optional host-memory packing requested from DeckLink for detected RGB input.
# AUTO preserves the established ARGB/r210/R12B defaults. The active device and
# mode are checked; unsupported alternatives fall back safely and are logged.
[decklink]
rgb_8bit_packing: AUTO
rgb_10bit_packing: AUTO
rgb_12bit_packing: AUTO

[queue.Normal]
queue_size: 32
lead_frames: 4
startup_preroll_frames: 0
# 5 holds 4 source frames ahead of the picture for the motion vectors (docs/VP-nvof-ahead.md).
target_frames: 5
# Active-picture look-ahead: absent = automatic, it follows the queue (queue = look-ahead):
# a normal profile looks min(target_frames - 1, queue_size - 1, 8) queued frames ahead, a
# low-latency profile 0. A number from 0 to 8 overrides it, including 0 (none).
#active_picture_lookahead_frames: 0
#reset_after_render_restart_seconds: 5
#reset_queue_too_large_percent: 70

shortcut: Shift+Q
[queue.Low_Latency]
queue_size: 1
lead_frames: 1
startup_preroll_frames: 0
target_frames: 1
# Active-picture look-ahead: absent = automatic, it follows the queue (queue = look-ahead):
# a normal profile looks min(target_frames - 1, queue_size - 1, 8) queued frames ahead, a
# low-latency profile 0. A number from 0 to 8 overrides it, including 0 (none).
#active_picture_lookahead_frames: 0
#reset_after_render_restart_seconds: 5
#reset_queue_too_large_percent: 75

reset_queue_too_large_percent: 200
shortcut: Shift+L
[directshow]
# Optional Input processing overrides. Omitted values inherit [general].
#video_conversion: V210_TO_P010
#container_colorspace: BT2020
#hdr_colorspace: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV
#hdr_luminance: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV
renderer_start_stop_time_method: CLOCK_SMART
frame_offset: AUTO
renderer_nominal_range: AUTO

[directshow.conversion]
conversion_method: SIMD
# Helper counts exclude the calling thread; omitted min/max both default to 1.
min_core_count: 1
max_core_count: 1
# AVERAGE (default) averages adjacent chroma rows.
# LEGACY takes zero-based even-row chroma and skips averaging.
# ADVANCED uses 12-tap Lanczos-3 vertical downsampling (more CPU; possible ringing).
chroma_downsampling: AVERAGE

[directshow.ppm]
ppm: AUTO

# Ordered LLDV profiles. The first profile is the default.
# DV profiles retain the lldv section name for compatibility.
# Each profile may choose true (DV Tunneling) or false (LLDV).
# Omitted mode inherits the first profile, then the legacy general flag.
[lldv.default]
#dolby_vision_tunnel: false
#max_cll: 1000
#max_fall: 401
#mastering_min_luminance: 0.001
#mastering_max_luminance: 4000

[logging]
#debug_log_retention: 10

# VP Renderer input overrides. Omitted values inherit [general].
#[vprenderer.input_processing]
#video_conversion: V210_TO_P010
#container_colorspace: BT2020
#hdr_colorspace: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV
#hdr_luminance: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV

# VP Renderer policy is independent from display calibration. Keep quality,
# tone mapping, scaling, and display precision here. Calibration stays in Color.
[vprenderer.Default]
# HDR-to-SDR tone-map destination only; SDR input levels are unaffected.
# HDR target white: above 0.000001 through 10000 nits, and above target black.
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
peak_detection: high_quality
contrast_recovery: 0.3
# Every libplacebo intermediate in 32-bit float (fbo_float32: auto, the
# default, = on everywhere, the low-latency path too); off saves GPU bandwidth at
# the cost of highlight precision (half-float rounds above white by up to
# 0.84 of a 10-bit step).
#fbo_float32: auto

# Hiccup repair (on by default, owner 2026-10-18): when a stream loses one to three
# frames, the frames either side are paired and the missing refreshes get pictures at
# their true time (untrusted areas hold). Separate from motion smoothing, which stays
# off (interpolation: off). off keeps the last frame on screen until the next arrives.
#interpolation_fill_gaps: on

[vprenderer.HDR]
when: $eotf=="pq" || $eotf=="hlg"
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
peak_detection: high_quality
contrast_recovery: 0.3

# Scaling, debanding and dithering belong to the Scaling profiles (v1.4.02 layout; Config
# moves older files here on load, with a backup). This file is already in that form, so it
# opens with no pending changes.
[vprenderer.scaling.Default]
upscaler: ewa_lanczossharp
downscaler: hermite
sigmoid: on
deband_strength: default
dithering: blue_noise
display_bit_depth: 10

# HDR sources (PQ / HLG, LLDV included): libplacebo's debanding off. In the
# HDR path its grain turns about 1 bright pixel in 10^4 black on each frame
# (measured on WARP and a GeForce, 2026-10-12; a libplacebo bug). 10/12-bit
# HDR has no source banding to remove, and output banding is the
# sigma-delta's job. SDR keeps debanding available (Default: default).
[vprenderer.scaling.HDR]
when: $eotf=="pq" || $eotf=="hlg"
deband_strength: off

# Playback monitor and complete output mode (Display page). Auto keeps the desktop
# resolution and the safe refresh-rate ranking.
[output_display.Default]
monitor: auto
mode_policy: auto
restore_previous_mode: true

# Color Config profiles select the calibrated Rec.709 or BT.2020 response.
# Each selection supplies display calibration and source-transfer handling
# without changing the Rendering profile or Screen Config.
[vprenderer.color.Rec709]
# One profile owns the display reference and its calibration files.
calibration_lut_enabled: false
calibration_lut_bt709: none
calibration_lut_p3_d65: none
calibration_lut_bt2020: none
# HDR output gamma before a usable LUT; inactive for SDR and without a LUT.
hdr_tone_map_target_gamma: 2.2
# Optional SDR encoding into the LUT, independent of HDR. No conversion by default.
sdr_lut_input_gamma: passthrough
# Color and Output are selected together by this profile.
output_presentation: AUTO
output_range: full
output_transport_gamma: 2.2
diagnostic_allow_limited_g22: false
# Display calibration transfer. This controls VP's color-managed render target;
# it does not request a different Windows or HDMI RGB transport.
output_gamma: 2.2
sdr_input_transfer: 2.4
target_primaries: REC709
report_bt2020_to_display: false
# Without a usable LUT, ON converts the expected SDR response to display gamma.
# PASSTHROUGH disables that gamma adjustment. With a usable LUT, the separate
# sdr_lut_input_gamma controls encoding; sdr_input_transfer always describes decoding.
sdr_adjust_gamma: passthrough

shortcut: F5
[vprenderer.color.BT2020]
# Display calibration transfer. Set a measured pure-power value (for example
# 2.2) only when this display profile is calibrated to that response.
output_gamma: 2.2
sdr_input_transfer: 2.4
target_primaries: BT2020
report_bt2020_to_display: true

shortcut: F6

[shortcuts]
# false keeps the existing global shortcut behavior. When true, VP processes
# shortcuts only while its presentation window is in the foreground and makes
# one bounded focus request after startup, renderer changes, and Config handoff.
# Shortcuts act only while VP (its window or its fullscreen picture) has focus.
# global_actions lists the actions that also act while another application
# (MPC-HC, a browser) has focus, e.g. "global_actions: config_editor"; empty or
# absent = none. Built-in defaults are Ctrl/Alt combinations; Esc exits
# fullscreen only while fullscreen is on and VP has focus.
foreground_only: false
render.1: Shift+A

# Subtitle profiles are independent of Screen and Zoom. Positive source-pixel
# offsets move bottom cues up / top cues down. Old subtitle keys elsewhere are ignored.

type: classic

cycle_shortcut: Ctrl+Shift+T

type: generated_gray
offset_pixels: 15

subtitle_text_color: FFFFFF
subtitle_generated_gray_color: 040404
subtitle_generated_gray_opacity: 0.85
subtitle_generated_gray_blur_px: 30

[vprenderer.subtitles.Black]

### sample docs/cinema/VideoProcessor-test.cfg
# VideoProcessor test configuration for the FL35 cinema (Stage 2)
#
# Launch with:  VideoProcessor.exe /config VideoProcessor-test.cfg
# Built from docs/VP-dither-judder-plan.md section 8. Like-for-like with the
# old madVR setup where the fork has an equivalent; differences are noted.
#
# The three custom shaders at the end start DISABLED. Enable them one at a
# time in the Config app (Standard shaders list, right-click > Enable in VP).

[general]
bounded_invalid_capture_recovery: true
# Pick the DeckLink input once in Config; it is saved back into this file.
#capture_device: <your DeckLink device>
renderer: VP Renderer
hide_legacy_renderers: true
fullscreen: true
windowed_fullscreen_mode: false
noui: false
startminimized: false
scene_detect: false
# The FL35 must stay at 60 Hz (48 Hz crops, 72 Hz is refused).
switch_refresh_rate: false
profile_change_display_seconds: 5
# v210 4:2:2 straight into the VP Renderer; no P010 conversion (plan constraint 8).
video_conversion: none
hdr_colorspace: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV
hdr_luminance: FOLLOW_INPUT_LLDV

[decklink]
rgb_8bit_packing: AUTO
rgb_10bit_packing: AUTO
rgb_12bit_packing: AUTO

# Queue profiles from the release sample, plus the Stage 5 time target.
[queue.Normal]
queue_size: 32
lead_frames: 4
startup_preroll_frames: 0
# Used until the source rate is known (and if target_latency_ms is removed).
target_frames: 5
# Stage 5: the queue target as time. VP turns it into whole source frames
# for each rate: 24p 5 frames (209 ms), 25p 5 (200 ms), 30p 6 (200 ms),
# 50p 10 (200 ms). Owner 2026-10-10: 200 ms (was 3 frames = 125 ms at 24p),
# so Dynamic Drive sees ~167 ms ahead; the audio delay goes up ~84 ms, once.
# 0 or no line = off (target_frames is used).
target_latency_ms: 200
# Absent = automatic (follows the queue: min(target_frames - 1, queue_size - 1, 8); 0 on a
# low-latency profile); a number from 0 to 8 overrides it, including 0.
# Queued frames the renderer inspects ahead of the current one (crop proofs
# and the LED lookahead), up to what the queue really holds: with 5 frames
# queued at 24p that is 4 future frames.
active_picture_lookahead_frames: 8
shortcut: Shift+Q

# Selected automatically for sources above 59 fps (games, 60p video), like the
# madVR fps rule (${cadence} is the exact rate: 59.94 and 60 both qualify;
# ${source_rate} is the rounded rate: 59.94 reads as 60). Shift+L still switches by hand.
[queue.Low_Latency]
when: ${cadence} > 59
queue_size: 1
lead_frames: 1
startup_preroll_frames: 0
# Its own target_frames beats Normal's target_latency_ms (not inherited).
target_frames: 1
# Explicit 0: otherwise this profile inherits Normal's look-ahead (8);
# with a 1-frame queue there is nothing to look at.
active_picture_lookahead_frames: 0
reset_queue_too_large_percent: 200
shortcut: Shift+L

[directshow]
renderer_start_stop_time_method: CLOCK_SMART
frame_offset: AUTO
renderer_nominal_range: AUTO

[lldv.default]

[logging]

# Display precision and HDR-to-SDR target. The DVI link to the FL35 is 8-bit.
[vprenderer.Default]
display_bit_depth: 8
# Every libplacebo intermediate in 32-bit float (exact highlights; more GPU
# bandwidth). auto = on, off with the Low_Latency queue. Turn off if the
# stats overlay shows render-time pressure on the 3090.
fbo_float32: auto
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0

# HDR sources (PQ / HLG, LLDV included): libplacebo's debanding off. In the
# HDR path its grain turns about 1 bright pixel in 10^4 black on each frame
# (measured on WARP and a GeForce, 2026-10-12; a libplacebo bug). 10/12-bit
# HDR has no source banding to remove, and output banding is the
# sigma-delta's job. SDR keeps debanding available (Default: auto).
[vprenderer.HDR]
when: $eotf=="pq" || $eotf=="hlg"
deband: off

# LLDV and HDR sources: static curve, no per-frame peak detection.
# contrast_recovery is a starting value; madVR's "Log Medium-High" has no
# exact equivalent (open question in the plan).
[vprenderer.input.Cinema]
tone_mapping: spline
peak_detection: off
gamut_mapping: auto
contrast_recovery: 0.3
sdr_input_transfer: 2.4
# madVR "Color & gamma: saturation +5".
saturation: +5
# Stage 6 de-judder and occlusion mask: off until tested (tally 32-38).
# Gentle is 0.25, Medium 0.35. The mask needs the sigma-delta shader.
#dejudder_strength: 0.25
#occlusion_mask: on
# Refinements (docs/VP-build-plan-refinements.md). These are the defaults
# (D1-D4 and the grain-aware dither carry on, the occlusion fill off); they
# act only with the de-judder or the grain map on. Uncomment one and set it
# off (or 1 for the dither scale) for an A/B at the projector.
#dejudder_global_prior: on
#dejudder_vector_smooth: on
#dejudder_motion_scale_lo: 0.25
#dejudder_motion_scale_hi: 1.5
#dejudder_trust_scale: 0.6
#dejudder_occlusion_fill: off
#dither_grain_tolerance_scale: 3
# Stage 8b, the upscale blend for 1080p and below (docs/VP-stage8b-upscale-
# blend.md): for the cinema check, CNN alone, then uncomment to compare.
#adaptive_upscale: on
#adaptive_upscale_w_base: 1.0
#adaptive_upscale_superres_scale: on
#upscale_light: linear
# Stage 10: BTB/WTW kept through scaling (this chain: 2.4 in, 2.2 out, P3).
# Off until the projector check (235->255 ramp, PLUGE 4/12/16/20).
#preserve_extended_range: on

# Baseline dither for the sigma-delta comparison. madVR used Error Diffusion
# option 2; Floyd-Steinberg is the closest standard error diffusion here.
[vprenderer.scaling.Cinema]
dithering: error_diffusion_floyd_steinberg
# SSimDownscaler is designed to follow a Mitchell-Netravali downscale.
downscaler: mitchell

# Projector RealColor P3, film gamma 2.2 on the projector. VP now renders
# for the projector's real 2.2; the deliberate "gamma boost" (madVR's pure
# power 2.40 on a 2.2 display) lives in [led_drive.Default] gamma_boost and
# applies whether or not the LED module is enabled. Limited (video) levels;
# the GPU stays at full range and the projector input level stays "video".
# BT.2020 sources are gamut-mapped into P3 by VP (plan constraint 9).
[vprenderer.color.FL35_P3]
calibration_lut_enabled: false
calibration_lut_bt709: none
calibration_lut_p3_d65: none
calibration_lut_bt2020: none
output_presentation: AUTO
output_range: limited
output_gamma: 2.2
sdr_input_transfer: 2.4
target_primaries: p3_d65
report_bt2020_to_display: false
sdr_adjust_gamma: passthrough

# Black-bar zoom, like madVR's zoom169 / zoom1 profiles, on top of whichever
# screen profile is active. Switch it in the Config app: VP Renderer > Zoom,
# select a profile, press "Use in VP" (VP remembers the choice). Starts off.
[vprenderer.zoom]
label: Off (whole picture)
automatic_crop: false

# Both madVR profiles: detect black bars baked into the video and fit the
# picture without them (small bar changes are ignored).
[vprenderer.zoom.black_bars]
label: Black bars removed
automatic_crop: true

# zoom169's "reduce big bars": 16:9-ish films (1.7:1 and wider) also fill
# the screen width, trimming a little top and bottom. madVR removed only
# half of a big bar; this removes all of it.
[vprenderer.zoom.fill]
label: Black bars removed, fill width
automatic_crop: true
crop_narrower_content_to_fill_screen: true

# Custom GLSL shaders for the VP Renderer. The root entry makes "Off" the
# default; members have no shortcut or when rule, so all start disabled.
[shader.standard]
type: multi
label: Off

# Chroma upscaling from luma (Artoriuz CfL, MIT) as the stand-in for madVR NGU.
# Runs only for 4:2:2 / 4:2:0 sources.
[shader.standard.cfl_chroma]
shader_type: custom
label: CfL chroma upscaling
stage: pre_resize
order: 10
glsl_file: CfL_Prediction.glsl

# igv SSimDownscaler (LGPL-3.0), as madVR's "SSIM 2D 100%". Acts only while
# downscaling (4K sources to 1600 lines).
[shader.standard.ssim_downscaler]
shader_type: custom
label: SSimDownscaler
stage: post_resize
order: 20
glsl_file: SSimDownscaler.glsl

# Image upscaling for sources shorter than the output (1080p and below), the
# open stand-ins for madVR NGU Sharp. Enable at most ONE of FSRCNNX / ArtCNN.
# Once enabled, each shader engages by itself only when it applies: the two
# luma doublers when the picture is enlarged at least 1.3x vertically,
# SSimSuperRes whenever the source is shorter than the output, and the
# SSimDownscaler above only when it is taller. They act on YUV video (the
# DeckLink's normal capture), not on RGB input.
[shader.standard.fsrcnnx]
shader_type: custom
label: FSRCNNX upscaler
stage: pre_resize
order: 15
glsl_file: FSRCNNX_x2_16-0-4-1.glsl

[shader.standard.artcnn]
shader_type: custom
label: ArtCNN upscaler
stage: pre_resize
order: 16
glsl_file: ArtCNN_C4F16.glsl

# Optional refinement after upscaling (igv SSimSuperRes).
[shader.standard.ssim_superres]
shader_type: custom
label: SSimSuperRes (upscale refinement)
stage: post_resize
order: 25
glsl_file: SSimSuperRes.glsl

# Temporal sigma-delta quantizer (brief section 8). While it is enabled VP
# switches its own dither off for that frame; disable it to compare against
# error diffusion. param_enabled 0 = plain rounding (A/B), param_test_ramp 1 =
# 10-bit grey ramp for the stage 3 photos.
[shader.standard.sigma_delta]
shader_type: custom
label: Temporal sigma-delta
stage: post_resize
order: 90
glsl_file: Temporal sigma-delta.glsl
param_enabled: 1
param_levels_full: 0
param_swapchain_bits: 10
param_noise_amplitude: 0.15
param_motion_threshold: 1.0
param_test_ramp: 0

# Dynamic Drive (brief Stage 7, docs/VP-led-drive-module.md). OFF: nothing is
# sent to the projector until enabled is true AND mode is script or
# compensate. Close the old beta_22.py script first - only one program can
# use the COM port.
[projector.Default]
protocol: barco_ascii
port: COM1
baud: 19200
# Time between commands. 200 ms is what the old script ran for years;
# Barco documents at least 500 ms (the conservative value). Optionally a
# pause of burst_pause_ms after every burst_commands (Barco: 5 s after 20;
# 0 = no pause).
min_command_gap_ms: 200
# Largest LED drop in one command, so the black floor never falls in big
# steps (later commands continue the ramp); brightening is not limited.
max_levels_per_command: 12
burst_commands: 0
burst_pause_ms: 5000
# off = send only, never wait for or ask the projector anything (the old
# script's way, its exact bytes). on = wait for each reply, ask the
# projector its range, log lost commands; the keys below then apply.
read_back: off
# With read_back on: auto = ask the projector (LPW1 ?N / ?M); or a level
# 0-255. With read_back off, light stays within led_floor..led_ceiling.
light_min: auto
light_max: auto
ack_timeout_ms: 300
echo: on
# With read_back on: auto tries a plain carriage return, then the letters
# "cr" plus a carriage return, then falls back to the old script's bytes.
terminator: auto
# Command sent to light changed on screen, in ms (measure once with a camera).
# The gain switches this long after each LED command so the two land together.
led_latency_ms: 80
# Relative light output per LPW1 level, measured with a meter (lpw:flux,
# ascending). Absent = output proportional to LPW1.
#flux_table: 60:0.55, 80:0.78, 100:1.00

[led_drive.Default]
# The core Dynamic Drive settings - the Config app's Dynamic Drive page edits these.
# Everything else (curve, step tables, ramps, highlight timings, thresholds)
# is built in, matching the old beta_22 script; to change it, point
# profile_file at an LED profile (see docs/cinema/LED-PROFILE-GUIDE.md and
# led-profile-example.cfg). Settings here always win over the profile.
enabled: false
# log_only | script | compensate
mode: log_only
# Lowest LED level in dark scenes (60-255; the script used 100) and in fades
# to black (60 is the lowest LPW1 reaches).
led_floor: 100
led_floor_black: 60
# Lowest LED level when VP cannot see ahead (Low_Latency queue).
led_floor_reactive: 100
# mode: compensate: 0.33 medium, 0.15 strong protection, 0.6 light.
highlight_tolerance_stops: 0.33
# Gamma boost: encode as if for a 2.4 display on the 2.2 projector - the
# same lift as the old output_gamma 2.4. Applies even with enabled: false.
# off = no boost.
gamma_boost: 2.4
#profile_file: my-led-profile.cfg

[shortcuts]
foreground_only: false

### sample docs/cinema/led-profile-example.cfg
# LED profile - example. Every value below is VP's built-in default, which
# matches the old beta_22 script. To make your own profile: copy this file
# next to VideoProcessor-test.cfg (e.g. as my-led-profile.cfg), change the
# lines you want, delete the rest (anything left out uses the built-in
# value), then choose the file on the Config app's Dynamic Drive page (or set
# profile_file in [led_drive.Default]).
#
# Not in here, on purpose: enabled, mode, led_floor, led_floor_black,
# highlight_tolerance_stops and gamma_boost are set on the Dynamic Drive page
# and always win; the projector settings (COM port, led_latency_ms,
# flux_table, command timing) stay in [projector.Default] of the main file.
# See docs/cinema/LED-PROFILE-GUIDE.md for what each line does.

[led_drive.Default]

# --- Light level from picture brightness (both modes) ------------------
# APL code : LED level, rising. APL is the picture's average brightness in
# video codes (16 black, 235 white). The LED never goes below led_floor.
curve: 0:100, 20:100, 40:200, 70:230, 100:255, 255:255
# Never above this level (255 = full).
led_ceiling: 255

# --- Script mode: how the LED steps (the script's own tables) ----------
# Every tick_ms the LED moves one step towards its target; "distance:step",
# largest distance first. Brightening uses the dark table below
# steps_up_split_apl, the bright table above it.
tick_ms: 400
steps_up_dark: 100:7, 50:5, 25:4, 10:1, 0:1
steps_up_bright: 100:7, 50:4, 25:2, 10:1, 0:1
steps_up_split_apl: 40
steps_down: 100:5, 50:3, 25:2, 10:1, 0:1
# Small contrast changes are ignored within this band (the script's anti-pump).
anti_pump_band: 3
# A highlight for the script's contrast rules: the brightest block at or
# above this code.
script_highlight_code: 215
# Script mode sends the script's own LPW1 and CNTR commands, at its own
# times. On: VP also eases the picture across each whole contrast step
# (never brighter than the projector's own setting). Off = the script exactly.
script_smoothing: false

# --- Compensate mode: how the LED ramps --------------------------------
# LED levels per second when brightening / dimming, and target changes of
# this many levels or fewer are ignored while the LED is still.
rate_up: 400
rate_down: 120
deadband: 3
# Rate law (7c-1): the speed is proportional to the distance from the
# target, between rate_min and rate_max, so a ramp is fast while far away
# and settles as it arrives. "script" matches the script's step tables;
# "compensated" is a faster set to try once compensation is on. Any rate_*
# key below overrides the preset. rate_gain 0 = the fixed rate_up / rate_down.
rate_preset: script
# CNTR assist (7c-1): the projector's contrast supplies only what VP's gain
# cannot do without compressing highlights beyond the tolerance, so the LED
# can dim further in scenes with highlights. Off = VP's gain only.
cntr_assist: true
software_first: true
# The highest contrast the assist may use (100 = no limit beyond the
# projector's cntr_max_usable).
cntr_assist_max: 100
# Bright cut: drop the contrast to 50 on the cut and fade the picture up
# with the LED instead of holding the contrast high (off = held, descending
# with the LED). The fade-up follows the LED speeds above.
cut_cntr_dump: true
shaped_recovery: true
# The darkest start of a fade-up after a bright cut you accept (0.5 = half
# brightness). It sets a floor for the LED in every scene (about 102 at
# 0.5); the higher of it and led_floor applies. 0 = no floor from it.
max_cut_dip: 0.5
# A contrast left above 50 after a partial drop comes down with the LED,
# kept this many percent on the bright side.
recovery_bias_pct: 3
# The contrast a cut to a brighter scene goes to (59 = where your script's
# dump ended; never 50, since VP's gain cannot add white light). Default =
# dmd_clip_cntr in the projector settings.
#cut_cntr_level: 59
# How fast the picture fades up for what the LED and contrast cannot yet
# supply (the LED and contrast themselves keep the script's speeds).
recovery_rate_gain: 4.0
recovery_rate_min: 20
recovery_rate_max: 400
# Coming out of black with the look-ahead: the LED may lift to this level
# while the screen is still black (black given up for a shorter fade-up);
# 60 = keep the black absolutely, 255 = the old behaviour.
black_prelift_max: 120
# Start moving ahead of a cut the look-ahead has already seen.
early_ramp: true
# A cut the look-ahead has seen: one jump to the new scene's level, timed
# to land on the cut frame (both directions - the cut hides the change).
cut_jump: true
# A dark-to-bright cut it has not seen (Low_Latency): straight up in one
# command, like the old script's contrast dump, instead of ramping.
bright_cut_dump: true

# --- Compensate mode: highlights ---------------------------------------
# Soft-clip region just below white (fraction of the range).
knee: 0.08
# The "highlight" is this percentile of the picture's 3x3 blocks.
highlight_percentile: 99.5
# A highlight must last this long before it holds the LED up (flashes are
# ignored); protection relaxes over highlight_release_ms when it goes.
highlight_persist_ms: 250
highlight_release_ms: 2000
# Highlights smaller than area_small (fraction of the picture) are knee-
# compressed; larger than area_large they keep the LED up instead.
highlight_area_small: 0.005
highlight_area_large: 0.05
# The LED aims at the darkest APL of this window, so a flash never lifts it;
# a cut uses the new scene at once.
apl_persist_ms: 100

# --- Scene changes -------------------------------------------------------
# A cut: average block change of more than this many codes.
cut_threshold: 24
# A fade to black: APL falling for at least fade_ms at no less than
# fade_min_slope codes per second (any frame rate; the earlier fade_frames /
# fade_min_step are still read, as frames at 24p).
fade_ms: 250
fade_min_slope: 12

# --- Measurement ---------------------------------------------------------
# R, G, B weights for brightness. Looks backwards on purpose: these are the
# weights the old script really used (it read an RGB screenshot as BGR).
# True Rec.601 is 0.299, 0.587, 0.114.
apl_weights: 0.114, 0.587, 0.299
# The projector's light exponent on the final code: the FL35 light-meter fit
# (delta 4). The gamma boost no longer depends on it. Also on the Dynamic Drive
# page ("Projector gamma"); a value set there wins.
compensation_eotf: 2.41

### sample docs/examples/VideoProcessorRenderer.from-legacy.proposed.cfg
# VP-0028 port of the supplied legacy renderer configuration.
#
# Retained as a parser-validation fixture; production settings belong in the
# single VideoProcessor.cfg file.

# A migrated installation may use this typed NLS rule. Its target aspect comes
# from the selected viewport rather than a duplicated shader setting.
[shaders]
enabled: true
rules: nls,nls_off
default: none

[shaders.nls]
label: Nonlinear Stretch
shortcut: N
type: nls
file: NLS.hlsl
stage: pre_resize
geometry: protected
strength: 1.0
center_protection: 0.35
curve: 2.0
quality: medium
tolerance_percent: 5

[shaders.nls_off]
label: NLS Off
shortcut: n
output_aspect_ratio: native
none: true

[general]
persist_profile_selection: true
# This retains the legacy default if the optional refresh action template below
# is enabled deliberately during event-action acceptance testing.
event_action_delay_seconds: 5

# The legacy file has no automatic input/scaling profiles. Those groups are
# omitted, so they contribute no overrides and [vpvr.display] is inherited.

# F4 replaces display_rules_auto.  It clears the manual F5/F6 choice and
# returns this group to the base Rec.709 settings above.
[profile_groups.display]
profiles: rec709,bt2020
default: auto
when: $key=="F4"

[profile_groups.viewport]
profiles: normal,scope
default: normal

# The legacy manual-only $width==0 sentinel becomes real manual key selection.
[profiles.display.rec709]

sdr_target_primaries: REC709
report_bt2020_to_display: false

sdr_target_primaries: BT2020
report_bt2020_to_display: true

[profiles.viewport.normal]

# NLS requires an explicit screen_aspect because madVR owns display geometry.

[profiles.viewport.scope]

screen_aspect: 2.35:1
automatic_crop: false
# Optional fill threshold; omit for aspect-preserving fit. A current trusted

# crop_wider_content_to_fill_screen: true
# crop_wider_content_aspect_limit: 2.76:1
subtitle_fit: true
subtitle_hold_seconds: 2
subtitle_padding_pixels: 30

# The supplied legacy refresh command map was commented out, so no command is
# enabled by this port. This is its unified equivalent should it later be enabled:

#actions: audio_delay_film,audio_delay_video

#[event_actions.audio_delay_film]
#on: refresh.applied,refresh.confirmed
#when: $actual_refresh==23.976 || $actual_refresh==24 || $actual_refresh==29.97 || $actual_refresh==30
#program: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat
#arguments: 315

#

#on: refresh.applied,refresh.confirmed
#when: $actual_refresh==50 || $actual_refresh==59.94 || $actual_refresh==60
#program: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat
#arguments: 315

# Unconditional built-in-renderer settings migrated from legacy [display].

### sample docs/examples/VideoProcessorRenderer.legacy.cfg
# VideoProcessor built-in renderer configuration
#
# Place this file beside VideoProcessor.exe. The optional plugin and its private
# dependencies belong in the libplacebo subdirectory. Renderer selection and
# shader rules remain in VideoProcessor.cfg.

# Optional in-process renderer. Output defaults to SDR Rec.709. HDR, PQ, HLG,
# and detected LLDV input are tone-mapped to the configured SDR target.
[display]
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
switch_refresh_rate: true
quality: high
tone_mapping: spline
gamut_mapping: perceptual
peak_detection: AUTO
contrast_recovery: AUTO
upscaler: AUTO
downscaler: AUTO
deband: AUTO
dithering: AUTO
display_bit_depth: AUTO

# Presentation and renderer output encoding. COMPOSED uses the stable bitblt/DWM
# path. AUTO normally does the same, but selects a flip/direct-eligible candidate
# when LIMITED is requested. DIRECT always requests the flip candidate. Windows
# still decides the live presentation mode.
# AUTO range preserves Full/sRGB. LIMITED maps RGB to 16-235 (64-940 at
# 10-bit) only when DXGI advertises and accepts the matching Studio P709 mode.
output_presentation: DIRECT
output_range: FULL
# This is the calibrated display target, not the Windows RGB transport
# declaration. Full RGB uses the standard nominal sRGB swapchain while VP
# targets AUTO/sRGB, BT.1886, or any listed measured pure-power response.
# Limited RGB is a transport diagnostic: only AUTO/2.4 and the explicit 2.2
# experiment have matching supported DXGI declarations.
output_gamma: AUTO
# Display-target primaries and GPU reporting are independent. BT2020 always
# renders pixels for BT.2020 when selected. report_bt2020_to_display additionally
# asks an NVIDIA GPU to signal BT.2020 in the HDMI AVI InfoFrame through NVAPI.
# If that request is disabled or fails, BT.2020 rendering continues so the
# display gamut can still be selected manually. NVIDIA signaling should be used
# with caution.
# Keep these defaults in checked-in configuration. Deployment enables the F6
# profile below rather than changing the global default.
sdr_target_primaries: REC709
report_bt2020_to_display: false
# Controlled SDR source-transfer comparison. AUTO uses the capture EOTF mapping
# (BT.1886 for SDR). This is independent from output_gamma.
sdr_input_transfer: AUTO
# Current/legacy behavior is ON: honor the declared SDR input transfer and
# convert it to the accepted output transfer. AUTO is mpv-compatible for an
# automatic sRGB target; OFF suppresses SDR transfer conversion for comparison.
sdr_adjust_gamma: ON
# Diagnostic-only options. Readback samples the rendered 10-bit backbuffer once
# before presentation. Cache bypass leaves the persistent cache file untouched.
output_diagnostics: OFF
diagnostic_disable_shader_cache: OFF

# Optional presentation target. The aspect may be written as a decimal or
# width:height ratio.
screen_aspect: 2.35:1
# Treat meaningful non-black content inside
# either confirmed encoded black bar as content that must remain visible. The
# picture snaps up or down as required. No OCR/model path is used.
subtitle_fit: true
# Keep the exact move after bar content disappears. Every detection restarts
# this timer. Padding is added outside the detected content bounds.
subtitle_hold_seconds: 2
subtitle_padding_pixels: 30

# Optional display-calibration Cube. The matching target-gamut slot runs after
# DTM and target gamma, then Full/Limited range packing and dither follow it.
# calibration_lut_enabled: false
# calibration_lut_bt709: luts\Projector-Rec709.cube
# calibration_lut_p3_d65: luts\Projector-P3-D65.cube
# calibration_lut_bt2020: luts\Projector-BT2020.cube

# Optional manual display profiles. Checked-in configuration documents the
# deployed F5/F6 bindings but deliberately leaves them disabled. Deployment
# configuration enables the two shortcut= lines below. Both expressions are
# manual-only; VP does no source/content detection to select these profiles.
#
# Rule syntax uses ==, !=, <, <=, >, >=, &&, ||, ! and parentheses. Existing
# single: equality remains accepted for compatibility. Names and values are
# case-insensitive; quotes are optional.
#
# Available variables:
#   $source_rate   Truncated input rate (23.976=23, 59.94=59)
#   $eotf          SDR, HDR, PQ, HLG, UNKNOWN
#   $colorspace    REC_709, BT_2020, P3_D65, P3_DCI, P3_D60, etc.
#   $hdr_metadata  true or false (valid static HDR metadata is present)
#   $width, $height, $resolution (for example 3840x2160), $interlaced
#   $format        Capture pixel format, for example V210, UYVY, R10B or R12L
# Numeric rate/size conditions accept inclusive ranges, e.g. $source_rate==23-30.
# A manually selected profile remains active across source-state changes until
# another profile shortcut or display_rules_auto is selected.
[display_rules]
rules: rec709,bt2020

[display_rules.rec709]
# Manual-only: a capture width is never zero.
rule: $width==0
priority: 10
# Deployment: shortcut=F5
sdr_target_primaries: REC709
report_bt2020_to_display: false

[display_rules.bt2020]
# Manual-only: a capture width is never zero.
rule: $width==0
priority: 10
# Deployment: shortcut=F6
sdr_target_primaries: BT2020
report_bt2020_to_display: true

# Optional command launched after the renderer applies (or confirms) a display
# refresh rate. delay_seconds controls how long VP waits before it starts the
# command (default: 5; use 0 for no wait), allowing fullscreen/window state to
# settle. Keys use the truncated integer rate: 23.976 -> 23, 59.94 -> 59.
# Exact entries override wider ranges.
#[refresh_rate_commands]
#delay_seconds: 5
#23-25: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat 315
#29-30: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat 315
#50: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat 315
#59-60: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat 315

[shortcuts]
# Clears a shortcut-selected display rule and immediately returns to automatic
# [display_rules] selection.
display_rules_auto: F4

### sample docs/examples/VideoProcessorRenderer.unified.minimal.proposed.cfg
# Minimal unified renderer and viewport example.
#
# This deliberately small example exercises independent automatic input and
# scaling groups plus manual display and viewport groups. It omits LUTs and
# event actions and shaders so the core resolver behavior is easy to review.

priority: 100
upscaler: ewa_lanczossharp

[profiles.scaling.uhd]
when: $height>=2160
priority: 100
upscaler: AUTO

[profiles.display.rec709]
when: $key=="F5"
sdr_target_primaries: REC709
report_bt2020_to_display: false

[profiles.display.bt2020]
when: $key=="F6"

[profiles.viewport.normal]

# Omitted screen_aspect uses the VP Renderer output-panel aspect. DirectShow

screen_aspect: 2.35:1
automatic_crop: false
# Optional fill threshold; omit for aspect-preserving fit. A current trusted
# active picture, including a trusted full raster, can be cropped to fill.
# crop_narrower_content_to_fill_screen: true
# crop_narrower_content_aspect_limit: 2.20:1
# crop_wider_content_to_fill_screen: true
# crop_wider_content_aspect_limit: 2.76:1
subtitle_fit: true
subtitle_hold_seconds: 2
subtitle_padding_pixels: 30

[vpvr.display]
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
quality: high
tone_mapping: spline
gamut_mapping: perceptual
peak_detection: DEFAULT

### sample docs/examples/VideoProcessorRenderer.unified.proposed.cfg
# Unified renderer and viewport configuration example.
#
# Viewport state is resolved by VP before renderer/effect selection. The NLS
# rules therefore contain no physical-screen aspect of their own.

[shaders]
enabled: true
rules: nls,nls_protected,nls_off
default: none

[shaders.nls]
label: Nonlinear Stretch
shortcut: N
type: nls
file: NLS.hlsl
stage: pre_resize
geometry: classic
strength: 1.0
curve: 2.0
quality: medium
tolerance_percent: 5

[shaders.nls_protected]
label: Nonlinear Stretch Protected
shortcut: P
type: nls
file: NLS.hlsl
stage: pre_resize
geometry: protected
strength: 1.0
center_protection: 0.35
curve: 2.0
quality: medium
tolerance_percent: 5

[shaders.nls_off]
label: NLS Off
shortcut: n
output_aspect_ratio: native
none: true

[general]
# Profile selections persist by default. Any [profile_groups.<name>] section
# may set persist_profile_selection=false for session-only selection.
persist_profile_selection: true
# Event actions inherit this delay unless they specify delay_seconds themselves.
event_action_delay_seconds: 5

[profile_groups.input]
profiles: sdr,pq_hdr,hlg_hdr,lldv_like
default: auto
# This group-level key condition returns input selection to automatic mode.
when: $key=="Ctrl+F4"
# Ctrl+F7 is a diagnostic override and should not survive process restart.
persist_profile_selection: false

[profile_groups.scaling]
profiles: sd_hd,mid,uhd_native,performance
default: auto
when: $key=="Ctrl+F5"
# Ctrl+F8 is a diagnostic override and should not survive process restart.
persist_profile_selection: false

[profile_groups.display]
profiles: rec709_projector,bt2020_projector
default: rec709_projector

[profile_groups.output]
profiles: standard
default: standard

[profile_groups.viewport]
profiles: normal,scope
default: normal

# Input profiles: source metadata decides HDR/SDR processing.
[profiles.input.sdr]
when: $transfer==SDR
priority: 100
peak_detection: off
contrast_recovery: AUTO

[profiles.input.pq_hdr]
# Ctrl+F7 deliberately permits a manual PQ-processing override for diagnostic
# comparison even when the source is not currently signaled PQ.
when: $transfer==PQ || $key=="Ctrl+F7"
priority: 100
tone_mapping: spline
gamut_mapping: perceptual
peak_detection: high_quality
contrast_recovery: 0.25

[profiles.input.hlg_hdr]
when: $transfer==HLG
priority: 100
tone_mapping: spline
gamut_mapping: perceptual
peak_detection: high_quality
contrast_recovery: 0.25

[profiles.input.lldv_like]
# Streaming heuristic: SDR transfer in BT.2020 without valid static HDR data.
when: $transfer==SDR && $primaries==BT2020 && !$hdr_metadata
priority: 200
tone_mapping: spline
gamut_mapping: perceptual
peak_detection: high_quality

# Scaling profiles: capture geometry decides scaler/deband policy.
[profiles.scaling.sd_hd]
when: $height<=1080
priority: 100
quality: high
upscaler: ewa_lanczossharp
sigmoid: on
deband_strength: default

[profiles.scaling.mid]
when: $height>1080 && $height<2160
priority: 100
quality: high
upscaler: ewa_lanczos
sigmoid: on
deband_strength: light

[profiles.scaling.uhd_native]
when: $height>=2160
priority: 100
quality: high
upscaler: AUTO
sigmoid: off
deband_strength: off

# A session/manual comparison profile. Ctrl+F5 returns only the scaling group
# to automatic source selection; other groups keep their current selections.
[profiles.scaling.performance]
when: $key=="Ctrl+F8"
quality: fast
upscaler: bilinear
downscaler: bilinear
sigmoid: off
deband_strength: off

# Display profiles: manually selected calibrated output choices.
[profiles.display.rec709_projector]
# Key conditions are ordinary profile rules, not a separate shortcut feature.
when: $key=="F5"
sdr_target_primaries: REC709
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
output_gamma: 2.2
report_bt2020_to_display: false
calibration_lut_enabled: true
calibration_lut_bt709: luts\Projector-Rec709.cube

[profiles.display.bt2020_projector]
when: $key=="F6"
sdr_target_primaries: BT2020
sdr_target_nits: 100
sdr_black_nits: 0
output_gamma: 2.2
report_bt2020_to_display: true
calibration_lut_enabled: true
calibration_lut_bt2020: luts\Projector-BT2020.cube

# Output transport is independent from calibrated rendering profiles. It may
# rebuild the device/swapchain, while display profile changes stay live.
[profiles.output.standard]
output_presentation: DIRECT
output_range: FULL
output_transport_gamma: AUTO
output_diagnostics: OFF
diagnostic_disable_shader_cache: OFF

# Viewport profiles own the actual screen geometry to which the projector lens
# is fitted. Active content aspect is detected dynamically; it is not another
# configured viewport ratio. Profile names are labels, not behavior switches.
[profiles.viewport.normal]
when: $key=="F3"
# Omitted screen_aspect uses the VP Renderer output-panel aspect. DirectShow
# NLS requires an explicit screen_aspect because madVR owns display geometry.

[profiles.viewport.scope]
when: $key=="F2"
screen_aspect: 2.35:1
# Both fill settings use current trusted active-picture authority, including a
# trusted full raster. Omit either optional limit to allow that direction to
# crop any eligible content.
# crop_narrower_content_to_fill_screen: true
# crop_narrower_content_aspect_limit: 2.20:1
# crop_wider_content_to_fill_screen: true
# crop_wider_content_aspect_limit: 2.76:1
subtitle_fit: true
subtitle_hold_seconds: 2
subtitle_padding_pixels: 30

# Commands run only after a completed display refresh transition.
[event_actions]
actions: audio_delay_cinema,audio_delay_video,restore_audio

[event_actions.audio_delay_cinema]
on: refresh.applied,refresh.confirmed
when: $actual_refresh==23.976 || $actual_refresh==24
program: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat
arguments: 315

[event_actions.audio_delay_video]
on: refresh.applied,refresh.confirmed
when: $actual_refresh==50 || $actual_refresh==59.94 || $actual_refresh==60
program: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat
arguments: 285

[event_actions.restore_audio]
on: refresh.restored
when: $actual_refresh==60
delay_seconds: 0
program: C:\Videoprocessor\audio\audio_delay.bat
arguments: 0
